## Calibration

For each complete P match, the standard error is its Table P halfwidth divided by 1.96. The calculation uses the resulting ratios before display rounding. A zero halfwidth with zero Elo makes z undefined. A zero halfwidth with nonzero Elo gives the signed infinite limit.

| Match | Halfwidth | Standard error | z arithmetic | Status |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| P1 | 10.46 | 5.33673469 | `+2.78000000 / (10.46000000 / 1.96) = +0.52091778` | complete |
| P2 | 10.66 | 5.43877551 | `-0.69000000 / (10.66000000 / 1.96) = -0.12686679` | complete |
| P3 | 10.88 | 5.55102041 | `+1.91000000 / (10.88000000 / 1.96) = +0.34408088` | complete |
| P4 | 10.96 | 5.59183673 | `-0.69000000 / (10.96000000 / 1.96) = -0.12339416` | complete |

Q = (+0.52091778)² + (-0.12686679)² + (+0.34408088)² + (-0.12339416)² = 0.42106829.
With 4 complete P matches, the bounds are Q ≤ 9.49 and every |z| ≤ 2.50.
